### <span class="pt-pb" data-facs="6"></span>*AN* Alphabetical TABLE wherein is comprised the principal matter contained in the Commentary upon the Gospel of St. *MARK.*

#### A.

<p class="pt-ixterm" markdown="1">Account.</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">ALL men must give an *Account,* 905</p>

<p class="pt-ixterm" markdown="1">Accusation.</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Silence is the best Answer to a false *Accusation,* 1403</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">False *Accusation,* 1398, 1399</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">The most innocent are sometimes falsly accused, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/10-33/#p781">781</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixterm" markdown="1">Admonition.</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">When it should be severe, 1434, 1615 1616</p>

<p class="pt-ixterm" markdown="1">Adoption.</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">It belongs onely to Believers, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/9-7b/#p585">585</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Derived by the Son-ship of Christ, 911</p>

<p class="pt-ixterm" markdown="1">Advice.</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">It should precede our Actions, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/5-27b/#p278">278</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixterm" markdown="1">Adultery.</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Remedies against it, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/7-21/#p430">430</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixterm" markdown="1">Affections.</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">When they are sincere, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/4-16/#p208">208</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/6-20/#p338">338</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">When they are inordinate they are an hindrance, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/4-19/#p218">218</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Remedies against their inordinatenesse, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/4-19/#p218">218</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Christians are not required to be altogether without them, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/3-5/#p137">137</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixterm" markdown="1">Affliction. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/8-33d/#p532">532</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/10-31b/#p776">776</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/10-39/#p790">790</a>, 1317, 1320, 1349, 1490, 1491, 1518, 1519, 1533, 1539, 1590, 1604.</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Believers not exempt from it, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/2-23/#p124">124</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/2-25/#p128">128</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/5-23/#p272">272</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/4-35/#p239">239</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Fasting should be used in time of Affliction, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/2-19/#p123">123</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">God sometimes seems not to observe the *Affliction* of his Children. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/4-38/#p241">241</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/4-38b/#p244">244</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/6-48/#p379">379</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">In it we must trust in God, 1535</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Christ takes notice of the *Affliction* of his Servants. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/6-47/#p375">375</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">God sometimes deferrs help. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/6-48/#p377">377</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">God sometimes exerciseth his Servants with many *Afflictions* at once. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/6-50/#p382">382</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/7-25/#p442">442</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/10-46/#p802">802</a>, 916, 917</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">It is often greatest when deliverance is near. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/6-50/#p383">383</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">The Church in this World is exercised with it. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/6-47/#p376">376</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">God exerciseth Faith with it. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/7-27/#p447">447</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">It encreaseth Faith. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/7-28/#p453">453</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Christians should be prepared for it. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/8-31/#p514">514</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/8-34/#p534">534</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/8-34c/#p539">539</a>, 1227, 1281, 1282, 1286</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Such as follow Christ must suffer it. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/8-34c/#p538">538</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/10-38b/#p787">787</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Self-deniall a help to bear it. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/8-34b/#p537">537</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Prayer an Effect of it. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/10-47/#p803">803</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Following Christ in it, an evidence of love. 1387</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Prayer a Remedy against it. 1319</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">God tryeth his People with it. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/1-13/#p41">41</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/4-17/#p210">210</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">God gives a happy issue to it. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/1-13b/#p42">42</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/4-38b/#p245">245</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/7-29/#p455">455</a>, 1328</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Prayer, a Duty in time of *Affliction.* <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/1-40/#p85">85</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/3-10/#p148">148</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/10-47/#p803">803</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/10-47/#p804">804</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/10-49/#p807">807</a>, 1118</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">It is a stumbling-block to Hypocrites. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/4-17b/#p212">212</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Preservatives against the evil of it. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/4-17b/#p213">213</a>, 1282</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">In it we are apt to distrust God. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/4-38/#p243">243</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">The benefit of it, when sanctified. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/5-21/#p268">268</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/7-25/#p443">443</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/8-34d/#p541">541</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">They come to Christians by Providence. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/6-37/#p360">360</a>, 1259</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Difference between the Afflictions of the godly and the Afflictions of the wicked. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/3-27/#p370">370</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">The Circumstance of time sometimes aggravates it. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/6-46/#p374">374</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">It is twofold. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/6-47/#p376">376</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Christ is present with us in them. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/6-50/#p383">383</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Christ's Word a means to comfort in it. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/6-50/#p384">384</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">We must help the afflicted. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/6-53/#p388">388</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/6-53/#p389">389</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/7-31/#p458">458</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/7-31/#p459">459</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/8-1/#p466">466</a>, 1316</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Our Duty to bear it. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/8-34d/#p540">540</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/8-34d/#p541">541</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/8-34e/#p542">542</a>, 1080, 1171, 1331, 1384</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Christians therein conformable to Christ. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/9-12e/#p603">603</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/10-39/#p790">790</a>, 1312, 1322</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">We should not despaire of deliverance. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/9-18b/#p615">615</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Helps to prepare us for it. 1288, 1298</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">What Persons should be a Comfort to us in it. 1306</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Comfort in it to open our griefs. 1315</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">It is lawful to pray against it. 1322, 1323</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Helps to make us willing to suffer it. 1384</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Comfort against it. 1409</p>

<p class="pt-ixterm" markdown="1">Alms. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/10-21b/#p745">745</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">May be joyned with the publick Worship of God. 1046</p>

<p class="pt-ixterm" markdown="1">Ambition. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/10-18/#p731">731</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/10-37/#p785">785</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/10-41/#p793">793</a>, 923</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Naturally in men. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/9-33/#p652">652</a>, 1036</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Remedies against it. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/10-37/#p785">785</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/10-38b/#p787">787</a>, 1036</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Its evill Effects. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/10-41/#p793">793</a>, 1038</p>

<p class="pt-ixterm" markdown="1">Angels.</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">They guard Believers. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/1-13b/#p43">43</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">And comfort them. 1589</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">They shall attend on Christ at his second coming. 1158</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">They must gather the elect, 1158, 1159</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">The Saints shall be like them. 971</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Why they formerly appeared. 1582, 1583 1584, 1585</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">We should honour them. 1586</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Their Power. 1588</p>

<p class="pt-ixterm" markdown="1">Anger.</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Rash *Anger* sinful. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/7-11/#p414">414</a>, 1376</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Remedies against rash Anger. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/7-11/#p414">414</a>, 1376</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Anger against Sin. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/10-14/#p720">720</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/10-14b/#p721">721</a>, 1413</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Rules concerning Anger against Sin. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/10-14/#p720">720</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">All Anger is not unlawful. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/3-5/#p137">137</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Rules concerning it. ibid.</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Anger against Sin should be joyned with sorrow. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/3-5/#p138">138</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixterm" markdown="1">Apostacy. 1234</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Signs of it. 1235, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/3-19/#p159">159</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">The danger of it. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/10-31b/#p775">775</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixterm" markdown="1"><span class="pt-pb" data-facs="7"></span>Apostles. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/10-28/#p765">765</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">A part of Christ's Family. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/3-19b/#p160">160</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Whether lived upon Christ's Charges. ibid.</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Were immediately sent of Christ. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/6-7/#p307">307</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Had the gift of working Miracles. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/6-7/#p309">309</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/11-23/#p857">857</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Why Christ forbade them to provide for their journey. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/6-7/#p310">310</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Order and precedence among them. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/8-29/#p507">507</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">They could not alwayes work Miracles. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/9-18b/#p615">615</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Christ's care of them. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/10-32c/#p778">778</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">How they obtained the gift of Miracles. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/11-23/#p857">857</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">They lived in troublesome times. 1166</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Not distinguished from others by their habit. 1430</p>

<p class="pt-ixterm" markdown="1">Apparel. 1038, 1430</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Rules concerning it. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/1-6/#p21">21</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/1-6/#p22">22</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixterm" markdown="1">Apparitions.</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">We should not fear the Apparition of the Devill. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/6-49/#p381">381</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/6-50/#p382">382</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixterm" markdown="1">Ascension of Christ.</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Causes of it. 1655, 1656</p>

<p class="pt-ixterm" markdown="1">Authority.</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">It is not to be despised. 1566</p>

#### B.

<p class="pt-ixterm" markdown="1">Baptism. 1630, 1631, 1636, 1638</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">THe Excellency of it. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/1-9/#p28">28</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/11-30/#p879">879</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Its necessity. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/1-4/#p12">12</a>, 1637</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Administration thereof belongs onely to Ministers. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/1-4/#p13">13</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Why called *Baptism* of Repentance. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/1-4b/#p15">15</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">The ends of it. 1632</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">It seals Remission of Sins. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/1-4b/#p16">16</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">It was formerly administred by Dipping. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/1-5/#p19">19</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Why Christ was baptized. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/1-9/#p28">28</a>,</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Whether sprinkling be warrantable <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/1-9/#p29">29</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Infants have a Right to it. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/10-14b/#p722">722</a>, 1634, 1635</p>

<p class="pt-ixterm" markdown="1">Believers. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/10-33/#p780">780</a> 1351</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Christ's care of them. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/8-1/#p467">467</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/8-1/#p468">468</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/9-42/#p673">673</a>, 1090, 1091, 1092, 1219, 1293, 1294, 1347</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Adoption belongs onely to them. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/9-7b/#p585">585</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">They shall but taste of Death. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/9-1b/#p565">565</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">They must be like Children. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/9-36/#p657">657</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/9-42/#p673">673</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/10-15/#p723">723</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/10-15/#p724">724</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Suffer Afflictions. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/2-19/#p121">121</a>. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/2-23/#p124">124</a>. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/2-25/#p128">128</a>. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/3-22/#p164">164</a>. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/4-35/#p239">239</a>. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/4-38/#p241">241</a>. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/4-38b/#p244">244</a>. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/5-23/#p272">272</a>. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/5-25/#p273">273</a>. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/5-35/#p288">288</a>. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/6-8/#p311">311</a>. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/6-35/#p358">358</a>. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/6-48/#p379">379</a>. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/6-50/#p382">382</a>. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/6-50/#p383">383</a>. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/7-25/#p442">442</a>. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/10-38b/#p787">787</a>. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/10-46/#p802">802</a>. 916. 917. 1083. 1091. 1093. 1097. 1171. 1233. 1388. 1530. 1531. 1534.</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">They desire the use of the Sacraments. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/1-5/#p19">19</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Excellency of them. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/1-7/#p24">24</a>, 1274</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">The Devill will assault them. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/1-12/#p35">35</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/1-13/#p41">41</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Angels guard them. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/1-13b/#p43">43</a>, 1158</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">They endeavour to convert others. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/2-15/#p113">113</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">May be in the Company of the Wicked. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/2-16/#p114">114</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Their Conversation. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/2-19/#p122">122</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Must not seek their own Honour. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/3-11/#p150">150</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">God honours them. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/3-16/#p155">155</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Whether they may fall away. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/3-19/#p158">158</a>, 1436</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">They have their infirmities. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/3-31/#p177">177</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/4-10/#p189">189</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/9-38/#p663">663</a>, 1090, 1421</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">They are of spirituall kindred with Christ. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/3-34/#p180">180</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/3-34/#p181">181</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">They ought to instruct others. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/4-20b/#p224">224</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/5-14/#p260">260</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Shall be partakers of Glory. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/4-27/#p233">233</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Their Innocence strikes terrour into the Wicked. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/6-14/#p326">326</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/6-20b/#p339">339</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">God alloows them Rest from Duty. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/6-30/#p353">353</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Their Compassion. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/6-35/#p359">359</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">They do not alwayes enjoy Comfort. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/6-47/#p375">375</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Christ takes notice of their troubles. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/6-47/#p375">375</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/6-48/#p377">377</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Their Sins open the mouths of the enemies of Religion. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/7-3/#p395">395</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">They value the smallest Mercies of God. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/7-28/#p453">453</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">They should be prepared for tryals. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/8-31/#p514">514</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">The best of them are in part carnally minded. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/8-33c/#p531">531</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/10-35/#p784">784</a>, 1346, 1347</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Ignorance in the best of them. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/9-10/#p595">595</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Sometimes weak in Faith. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/9-22/#p626">626</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">They should be as spiritual Sacrifices. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/9-49/#p688">688</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Should be wise. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/10-2/#p699">699</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">They are often tainted with the Errours of the times. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/10-9/#p713">713</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Must be Disciples of Christ. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/10-21e/#p748">748</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Christ covers their infirmities. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/10-29/#p767">767</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">They shall be rewarded for their Sufferings. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/10-30/#p770">770</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/10-29c/#p771">771</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/10-30b/#p772">772</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Christ expects fruit of them. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/11-12/#p830">830</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">God fits them for difficult Work. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/11-23/#p857">857</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/11-23/#p858">858</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">God is their Father. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/11-25d/#p871">871</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">They must beware of false Teachers. 1034, 1062, 1081</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">They are the Glory of the Church. 1056</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Their flight a Sign of God's Wrath. 1110</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">God corrects them in this life more sharply than the Wicked. 1120</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">They must abstain from Sin. 1243</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Should grieve for the Sins of others. 1255</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">When suspected they must clear their innocency. 1256</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Their Ʋnion with Christ. 1274</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Are not free from Sin. 1289, 1298, 1294, 1298, 1351, 1363, 1374</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">The best not exempted from temptation. 1342, 1343</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Why God leavs some Corruption in them. 1346, 1421</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">God imployes the Wicked to afflict them. 1355</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Their Zeal is not alwayes according to Knowledge. 1375</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">It is a great Sin to injure them. 1418</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">They sometimes sin against knowledge and Conscience. 1422</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">They are sometimes fearfull in danger. 1423</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">They must expect to meet with unthankfull men. 1471</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">They ought to speak for Christ's Cause. 1472</p>

<p class="pt-ixterm" markdown="1">Benefactors.</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">We should not speak evill of them. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/9-37/#p660">660</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixterm" markdown="1">Benefits.</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Christ the ground of them. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/1-11/#p34">34</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixterm" markdown="1">Blasphemy.</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Sinfulness of it. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/3-28/#p172">172</a>, 1413, 1416</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">What it is. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/7-23/#p436">436</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Remedies against it. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/3-28/#p172">172</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/7-23/#p437">437</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Kinds of it. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/2-6/#p101">101</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/2-7/#p102">102</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/7-23/#p436">436</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">We ought to abhor it. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/2-7/#p102">102</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Committed four wayes. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/3-28/#p172">172</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixterm" markdown="1">Body.</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Its natural impurity. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/7-19/#p425">425</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixterm" markdown="1">Brothers.</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Why the Name of the eldest was to be continued. 958</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Why his Inheritance was to continue in his Name. 959</p>

<p class="pt-ixterm" markdown="1">Burial.</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Decent Buriall due to the dead. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/6-27/#p350">350</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/6-27/#p351">351</a> 1570</p>

#### C.

<p class="pt-ixterm" markdown="1">Calamities.</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">THe Calamities at the second coming of Christ. 1080</p>

<p class="pt-ixterm" markdown="1">Call.</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">No Office should be undertaken without it. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/1-38/#p83">83</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">When it is lawfull. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/1-10/#p30">30</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/1-38/#p83">83</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">God fits those whom he calleth. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/1-10/#p31">31</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/6-7/#p309">309</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Some great Persons are called. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/5-21/#p268">268</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Those that are called must free themselvs from hinderances. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/6-8/#p311">311</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/10-49/#p807">807</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">It ought to be obeyed. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/6-11c/#p320">320</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Sometimes Actions are successful though performed without a lawfull Call. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/9-38/#p663">663</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">The Power of it. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/10-28/#p764">764</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">How we may know it. 1066, 1375</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1"><span class="pt-pb" data-facs="7"></span> No Action should be undertaken without it. 1374</p>

<p class="pt-ixterm" markdown="1">Calling.</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">An Ordinance of God. 1194, 1195</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Christians should ply their own Calling. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/10-1/#p696">696</a>, 1195, 1511</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Calling should not be forsaken. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/11-27b/#p875">875</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Outward Calling a great Priviledge. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/7-27/#p448">448</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">All ought to have a particular Calling. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/6-3/#p300">300</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Religion may be practised in any lawfull Calling. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/6-3/#p301">301</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixterm" markdown="1">Care.</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">It is two-fold. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/4-17b/#p214">214</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">When immoderate it is a hindrance. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/4-18/#p215">215</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Remedies against immoderate Care. *ibid.*</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">When it is immoderate. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/6-37/#p362">362</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">We must not distract our selvs with it. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/8-14/#p480">480</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/8-16b/#p486">486</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Remedies against distracting Care. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/8-14/#p480">480</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixterm" markdown="1">Catechizing.</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">It is a Duty. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/8-29/#p506">506</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixterm" markdown="1">Censure.</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">It proceeds from malice. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/2-7/#p103">103</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Natural to us. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/2-18/#p120">120</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">The Property of an Hypocrite. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/2-23/#p126">126</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixterm" markdown="1">Ceremonies. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/2-25/#p129">129</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/7-3/#p394">394</a>, 1009</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">The Church may ordain outward Ceremonies. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/7-7/#p401">401</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixterm" markdown="1">Charity. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/3-1/#p135">135</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/3-1/#p136">136</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Its Works are to be preferred before outward Ceremonies. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/2-25/#p129">129</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Motives to it. 1047, 1222, 1223</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">It is acceptable to God. 1048</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Christ observs it. 1048, 1052</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Rich men should especially be charitable. 1049</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">It is a Duty. 1050</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">God judgeth it not by the gift, but by the heart. 1052, 1053</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Christians should use all occasions to express it. 1222</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Cautions concerning it. 1223</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">The heart must be free in it. 1224</p>

<p class="pt-ixterm" markdown="1">Children.</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">God sometimes makes them a Curse. 1114</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Should be nurst by the natural Mother. 1115</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Christians must be like them. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/10-15/#p723">723</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/10-15/#p724">724</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/9-36/#p657">657</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/9-42/#p673">673</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Christ's Love to them. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/10-16/#p725">725</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">We should pray for them. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/10-16/#p725">725</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">They are naturally tainted with Sin. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/9-21/#p624">624</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Their Duties to Parents. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/7-10/#p407">407</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/7-10b/#p410">410</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">They should not curse their Parents. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/7-10b/#p411">411</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">They ought to help their Parents. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/1-29/#p74">74</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/7-12/#p416">416</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixterm" markdown="1">Christ. 1224, 1230</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">He rules the Devils. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/1-23b/#p65">65</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/1-25/#p70">70</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Why the Devil professed his Knowledge of him. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/1-24/#p67">67</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/5-1/#p253">253</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Why Christ rebuked the Devill for confessing him. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/1-24/#p69">69</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Faith unites to him. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/5-34/#p285">285</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">God's Love to him. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/1-11/#p33">33</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/9-7c/#p586">586</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Christ the chief Teacher. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/3-27/#p370">370</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/7-14/#p417">417</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/9-7d/#p587">587</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/9-7f/#p591">591</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/10-9/#p713">713</a>, 1033</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">All ought to hear him. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/9-7e/#p588">588</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">His Enemies. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/3-22/#p163">163</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/6-53/#p390">390</a>, 1028, 1029, 1030, 1058, 1085, 1377</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">He is true God. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/1-1/#p4">4</a>. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/1-7/#p24">24</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/1-30/#p76">76</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/2-5/#p98">98</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">His Dignity. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/1-2/#p7">7</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/1-7/#p24">24</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/1-10/#p31">31</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/9-12/#p599">599</a>, 912</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">We must forsake all for him. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/1-9/#p28">28</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/1-17/#p58">58</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/5-23/#p271">271</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">He resembles the Dove. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/1-10/#p31">31</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">The ground of Ged's Love to us. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/1-11/#p34">34</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Why he was nempted forty dayes. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/1-13/#p38">38</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/1-13/#p39">39</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">His Love to us. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/1-13/#p39">39</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/2-25/#p127">127</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/8-1/#p467">467</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/8-1/#p468">468</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/8-33c/#p531">531</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/9-25/#p636">636</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/9-36/#p656">656</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/10-23b/#p756">756</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/10-33c/#p782">782</a>, 1311, 1332, 1335</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">He is Lord of all the Creatures. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/1-13b/#p42">42</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/4-39/#p246">246</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/6-48/#p378">378</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">He governs the Church. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/1-14b/#p46">46</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/1-14b/#p47">47</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">His Kingdom. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/1-14b/#p47">47</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/11-7/#p818">818</a>, 1027, 1550, 1564</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">His Power. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/1-21/#p60">60</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/1-41/#p89">89</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/4-38b/#p245">245</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/6-41/#p366">366</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/6-48/#p378">378</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/8-23b/#p497">497</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/9-38/#p663">663</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/11-1/#p813">813</a>, <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/11-4/#p815">815</a>, 1247, 1649</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">His Holiness. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/1-24/#p68">68</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">He can assume a Body. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/6-48/#p380">380</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">He can enter into men. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/9-17/#p612">612</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">He fits his Temptations to the time. 1129</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">We are naturally unable to do it. 1344</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Who come thither. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/10-14b/#p722">722</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">We should prevent them. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/5-37/#p289">289</a>. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/9-30/#p643">643</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixterm" markdown="1">Hypocrites. 1368. 1401</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">They maske their Sin in fair pretences. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/11-17d/#p845">845</a>. 1216. 1517</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">They go far in profession. 1016. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/10-21f/#p749">749</a>. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/7-3/#p394">394</a>. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/6-20/#p337">337</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">They make Religion a Cloak for Sin. 1042. 945</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Misery of them. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/11-14b/#p833">833</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">They rest in outward conformity. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/10-19/#p735">735</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">They are forward in propounding Questions. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/10-2/#p697">697</a>. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/10-2/#p698">698</a>. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/10-2/#p699">699</a>. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/8-10/#p471">471</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Have knowledg in the Scriptures. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/10-3/#p701">701</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">They contemn Ordinances. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/8-11/#p472">472</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Busily pry into others faults. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/7-1/#p392">392</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">It's their property to tye others to their manner of worship. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/7-3/#p393">393</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">They put holiness in outward Ceremonies. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/7-3/#p394">394</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">They add to God*'s* Word. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/7-3/#p395">395</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">They serve God after the Precepts of men. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/7-7/#p400">400</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Their shew it but for a time. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/4-17/#p209">209</a>. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/4-17b/#p213">213</a>. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/3-19/#p158">158</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">They are censorious. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/2-23/#p126">126</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">The Church is never without them. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/3-19/#p157">157</a> <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/4-11b/#p194">194</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Affliction is a stumbling-blook to them. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/4-17b/#p212">212</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">They stand much for old Customs. 1460. 1461</p>

<p class="pt-ixterm" markdown="1">Idolatry.</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">THe sinfulness of it. 1104</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Christ is able to cure it. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/8-24/#p499">499</a>. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/10-51/#p809">809</a>. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/11-18d/#p850">850</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixterm" markdown="1">Journeys.</p>

<p class="pt-ixterm" markdown="1">Indifferent.</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">In things indifferent we should conform to the Church. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/4-1/#p184">184</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">It is an effect of Sin. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/3-19/#p157">157</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixterm" markdown="1">Infants.</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Fear proceeds from it. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/4-39/#p248">248</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">The danger of it. 892</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Christ is privy to them. 1287</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">It is profitable for Christians to know them. 1287</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Christ covers the infirmities of his Servants. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/10-43/#p797">797</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">They are in the best. 1421</p>

<p class="pt-ixterm" markdown="1">Injuries. 925</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">We should forgive them before we pray. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/11-25/#p866">866</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">The Doer of them is more miserable then the Receiver. 1262</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Christ is ready to instruct us. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/10-9/#p713">713</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">He fell away by degrees. 1234</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">An unjust Judge. 1414</p>

<p class="pt-ixterm" markdown="1">Judgment.</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">The last Judgment. 1371</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">A day appointed for it. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/6-11c/#p319">319</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">God hath many of them. 1075</p>

<p class="pt-ixterm" markdown="1">Kindred.</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Kingdom of God is two-fold. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/4-11/#p191">191</a>. 1014</p>

<p class="pt-ixterm" markdown="1">Kings.</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">They are Servants of Christ. 1194</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">Believers sometimes sin against it. 1422</p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">The Devil is an Enemy to the Knowledg of of Christ. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/6-14c/#p329">329</a></p>

<p class="pt-ixsub" markdown="1">They should be lawfully called. <a href="{{ site.baseurl }}/petter/mark/3-13/#p152">152</a></p>
